The pool holds `PoolEntry.planProposal` from `plan_proposal` to that frame (`SidecarPool.pendingPlan`; an omp exit drops it) and forwards both to hidden tabs; every window's `use-plan-approval.ts` clears the card unless its own answer is in flight (that keeps its outcome notice). `ControlTab.plan` makes the session waiting everywhere asks do (`waits()` in `service.ts`: listing `waiting for plan approval: "<title>" (<path>)`, `ompdesk_session_result` wait, no auto-close, no merge, review item `waiting`). `_answer` on it sends the card's own `plan_approval`: `approve: true` with `choice` `execute` (default) / `compact` / `keep_context` (approving follows the tool-approval rule, `approveEnabled`, implied by full control), or `approve: false` with `text` as feedback (always allowed); `save` stays the person's. Pinned by `service.test.ts` (plan cases), `sidecar-pool.test.ts`, `PlanApprovalDialog.test.tsx` and the plan tests of `e2e/agent-control.e2e.ts` (the fake omp mirrors `settlePending`).
- A message another session's agent sends arrives as a stock prompt wrapped in `<ompdesk-agent-message from="…">` (`wrapAgentMessage`; a body cannot forge or close it), shown as "에이전트가 보냄 · title" (`MessageBubble`/`promptDisplayText`).
- The user can hide a session from agents (Sidebar session menu › "다른 에이전트가 이 세션을 보지 못하게", `agentHidden` in the `sidebar` prefs blob, this computer's sessions): it lists as hidden and refuses read, send, wait, answer, show and stop.
- Autopilot hooks (optional host deps `isStopping`, `beforeFirstPrompt`; `AgentControl.childrenOf`, `clearQueue`, `openSessions(caller, sessions, tag)`) keep `src/main/autopilot*.ts` out of this module.
- Tests: `agent-control/*.test.ts`, `shared/agent-control.test.ts` (default resolution: unset → manage on, approve off; stored `false`/`true` honoured), `AgentControlRow.test.tsx`, `e2e/agent-control.e2e.ts` (fake omp: create → send → wait → read, the delete card for a foreign session, an approval), `e2e/agent-app-control.e2e.ts` (no card by default, the card with the switch stored off, a deleting action asks either way), opt-in `e2e/real-agent-control.e2e.ts`. The fake omp implements `get_last_assistant_text`, `agent_control.confirm` and `agent_control.note`.

### App control (`ompdesk_app_list`, `ompdesk_app`)
- Changes the app itself in main; arguments are validated against the action's schema. `app-catalog.ts` actions, `app-settings.ts` one `settings.<row>` action per Settings row, `app-control.ts` the one dispatch point, `app-guard.ts` and `app-channels.ts` the agent's own default-deny risk tables. `AGENT_CHANNELS` is typed over every `IpcChannel`: a new channel does not compile until it is `never` with a reason or lists the actions that call it. `IPC_POLICY` is the web device's table, not the agent's.
- `read`/`change` are free; a change is journaled in the caller's session (`agent_control_note`) and shown as a quiet toast with one-step undo when a previous value exists (`AGENT_CONTROL_APP` → `lib/agent-app.ts`, applied live).
- `sensitive` raises no card while full control is on. With it off, it raises the same `[OMPdesk]` card only while `agentControlManage` (default on) is off, **except the actions whose `manage` rule is `always`** (`ManageRule` in `app-settings.ts`, a field of `AppAction` and of a Settings row): every `sensitive` action must decide `{ lifts: reason }` or `{ always: reason, when? }`; `app-catalog.test.ts` fails on one that does not, and `app-control.test.ts` pins the `always` list (`agent_power.set`, `fullOnly`, is pinned on its own). `always` asks whatever the manage switch says because the action erases data (`sessions.delete`, `memory.purge`, `memory.delete_page`, `memory.sweep`), runs code (`extensions.install_plugin`, `extensions.install_mcp`, `extensions.mcp_use`), touches credentials or traffic (`accounts.github_sign_in`, `accounts.provider_sign_in`, `extensions.mcp_sign_in`, `settings.proxy`), exposes or connects the computer (`mobile_web.set_enabled` when turning it on, `remote.connect`, `remote.unblock`, `remote.set_allowed_users`) or ends running work (`updates.apply`, `app.quit`). The rest lift (`updates.check`, the four auto-update switches, `builtinRea`, `rotation`, `remote.block`); an `undo` of a setting asks by its row's rule. `remote.list_tailnet` (read) lists the tailnet's computers for `remote.connect`. MCP servers: see Panels, sidebar and work views › MCP servers.
- **Which computer.** A caller tab that runs on a paired host (`AppPorts.device(tabId)` = its sidecar's `device`; `ActionContext.device`) gets what the Settings page gives that tab, so no action is local-only where a host route exists. Already the host's through the tab's own RPC: every stock setting, models, usage, plugins, MCP. Routed by device: `HOST_PREFS` (`app-settings.ts`) sends `settings.rotation` and `settings.builtinRea` (read, write and undo) through `ACCOUNT_ROTATION_*`/`BUILTIN_REA_*` with the device (`machine-services.ts` `RemoteMachine`), never into this computer's prefs; `sessions.saved|search|rename|delete` put the device in the `sessions:*` payload; `updates.status|check|apply` take `host: true` for `HOST_DESKTOP_*` (the host's OMPdesk; a session on this computer refuses it, and the host refuses an install while any work runs). Tiers, cards and the channel table are unchanged (`AGENT_CHANNELS` lists the routed channels per action). This device's own, in the Settings UI too, so they stay here: every other pref row (a paired host's tab follows the host's own pref), this app's updater switches, memory, remote devices, Mobile web, GitHub, `ui.*`, `app.quit`.
- `autopilot.*` actions call `ompdesk_autopilot` (its card and folder rules); `get_autopilot`/`set_autopilot` are `never` for app actions.
- `never` refuses, whatever the switches say: secrets and tokens, answering a card, `RPC_COMMAND_FOR_TAB` and any raw RPC passthrough. While full control is off it also refuses the agent-power switches (the control tools switch, `agentControlFull`/`agentControlManage`/`agentControlApprove`, `tools.approvalMode` and every approval, memory and computer-use switch), which `agent_power.set` reaches only under full control. It holds whether reached as a pref, a stock setting, an RPC command or one command inside another (`ActionContext` re-checks every primitive with the current full-control state; a nested RPC command is `never`).

### The `ompdesk` skill
- Kernel plus references: `SKILL.md` (at most 60 lines: tools, when to open a session, definition of done, the brief template with Ownership and Observable acceptance, handoff vs supervise, reuse a finished child, "silence is a checkpoint, not failure") links `skill://ompdesk/references/<file>.md` (`orchestration`, `worktrees-review`, `schedules`, `across`, `autopilot`, `app-control`), resolved against the skill's own `baseDir` inside an extension root (`internal-urls/skill-protocol.ts`). Each rule sits where it is used (e.g. never follow an absolute path in a child's answer into the main checkout: in the collect and compare steps). The kernel teaches the opener's loop (dispatch, end the turn, be woken, judge with `ompdesk_compare`, merge) instead of polling.
- `skill-drift.test.ts` fails when a tool or parameter in `tools.ts` and the skill disagree (renamed, added, or a word on a kernel tool line that is no parameter or enum value of it), when a `skill://` link names a missing file, or when the kernel passes 60 lines.
- There is no "remember" control tool: an agent saves durable learnings with ai-memory's own MCP tools (the skill points at `ai-memory-durable-pages`).

### Worker contract
- `#openOne` sends each child `agent_control_worker {from}` (local handler → bridge `agent_control.worker`, free in `quota-commands.ts`) once ready and before its first prompt. The child journals one `ompdesk.worker` custom entry; `resources/gui-bridge/agent-control.ts` registers the `ompdesk-worker` prompt section (`registerPromptSection`), appending `WORKER_CONTRACT` for the main agent of a session whose branch holds that entry.
- `WORKER_CONTRACT`: stay in your folder and the named scope; done = the message's acceptance checked; the final answer names changes, the check run and what is left, and ends with `Result: ok` or `Result: problem — <reason>`; report done/question/blocked with `ompdesk_report` instead of waiting to be asked.
- Journal-based, so a relaunched child keeps it; a session the user opened, the parent and a worker's own subagents never get it. Pinned by `bridge-agent-worker.test.ts` and `service.test.ts` (fan-out); the fake omp answers `agent_control.worker`.

### Worktrees and the review list
- `ompdesk_session_open` with `worktree` makes each child's checkout and branch through `worktree_create` (`omp/agent/<slug>`, base `HEAD` or `default`). Files: `branches.ts`, `stores/agent-review.ts`.
- **An open never leaves half** (v0.46.0 left a worktree and branch behind when a busy omp's first command passed 8 s): the child's first command (`agent_control_worker`, which waits for the local handler's `get_state`, the bridge connect and the bridge's module load) gets `FIRST_COMMAND_TIMEOUT_MS` (60 s, `service.ts`); an open that still fails, or a spawn that never started, closes the tab and, once the tab is gone from the pool (`CLOSE_WAIT_MS`; a tab the window keeps keeps its worktree, named in the error), takes back the worktree it made through `discardOpenedWorktree` (`disk/saved-worktrees.ts` over `discardNewWorktree` in `branches.ts`; `host.git.discardWorktree` on a paired host): under `withRemovalClaim` with every open folder of this computer (`sessionOpenFolders`; no tab starts there meanwhile), once the closed tab's omp has exited (`omp-exits.ts`: `SidecarPool` and `LocalHostServices` note every released omp's exit by folder; up to 5 s, else kept) and, where process folders can be read (not on Windows), no other process works in it, `archiveBeforeRemove` first, as before every removal (ignored files a seeding or setup hook made or someone edited are archived and listed under Saved worktrees; a refusal keeps the worktree), then `worktree remove` without `--force`, then `update-ref -d` of the branch against the commit it was created at, only while its reflog holds that one entry. A branch cut from the default branch is ahead of an unpulled parent checkout, so `removeSafeWorktree`'s `branch -d` would keep it. What git keeps is named in the error. Pinned by the "8 s", "takes back the worktree" and "tab is still open" cases of `service.test.ts` and `discardNewWorktree` in `branches-worktrees.test.ts` (an edited ignored `.env` archived and restored; the claim held while it archives; a worktree in use or with a process in it kept).
- **On every computer alike** (the user's rule: every agent tool works on a paired device's sessions exactly as on local ones): the git of a session's worktree runs on the computer the session runs on. `AgentControlHost.branchFacts`/`mergeBranch`/`landing`/`removeSafeWorktree` take the session's `device`; for a paired host `host.ts` asks it `host.git.facts|evidence|landing|merge|removeWorktree` (`GIT_METHODS` in `remote/app-protocol.ts`, params zod-checked in `host-services.ts`, `GIT_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`), which run the same `branches.ts` on the host's checkout and answer the same shapes; a `BranchError` reaches the agent as its message. A child opened on another computer than the caller's (`device` ≠ the caller's) gets its worktree there: `host.git.worktreeCreate {baseCwd, name, branchPrefix, baseRef}` runs the same `createWorktree` (seeding and setup hook included) on the host, or locally for a remote caller opening here; it needs that device's absolute `folder`. No session of that computer has to run: the location comes from the stock helper's `git.worktree_location` (`stock-helper/worktrees.ts`: stock `worktree.base` read-only for the repository, applied with pi-utils `setWorktreesDir` as stock's effect does, then `getWorktreesDir`/`hashPath`; `tablessWorktreeBridge` in `local/git/worktree.ts`; internals under `worktreeLifecycle` in `capabilities.ts`), the answer a session's bridge gives. A caller on the target computer keeps the `worktree_create` command through its own tab. Git ops are free (no `PeerQuota`). Pinned by the paired-host cases of `service.test.ts` and `host.test.ts`, the `host.git.*` tests of `remote/host-services.test.ts` (real temp repos, no session running), `stock-helper-worktrees.test.ts` (real omp: config's `worktree.base`, `OMP_WORKTREE_DIR` over it, stock's hash) and `e2e/agent-control-across.e2e.ts` (two apps: worktree session on the host, compare, merge into the host's checkout; the fake install's stock modules are `e2e/worktree-location-fixture.ts`).
- `ompdesk_compare` reads each child's branch (`git merge-base`, `diff --numstat`, commits ahead, uncommitted and untracked counts), a merge prediction and its final answer; it **runs no tests** (a child reports them). The prediction (`predictMerge`, `branches.ts`) is `git merge-tree --write-tree --name-only` of the parent's HEAD and the branch tip with a quarantined object store (`GIT_OBJECT_DIRECTORY` a scratch dir, the repo's store as alternate, deleted after; idea from Orca's `conflict-summary.ts`, MIT, re-implemented), so no checkout, index, ref or object of the repo changes; the line reads `merge: would conflict in <files>`, `no conflict with <base>` or `not predicted (<reason>)` (git < 2.38), and a predicted conflict marks the child's review item `conflict`.
- **Where a branch lands** (`branchFacts`/`landingFacts`, one rule): the parent checkout's HEAD and the upstream its branch tracks (`<branch>@{upstream}`, else `refs/remotes/origin/HEAD`; refs as last fetched, never fetches, never touches the parent checkout). Agents push their own work (`git push origin HEAD:main`) while the parent's local main stays behind, so a tip in either has **landed** (`BranchFacts.merged`) and its diff is measured from that tip (only uncommitted work is left). An unlanded branch is measured from its merge-base with the upstream when the upstream holds the parent's HEAD, else with the parent's HEAD, so a stale local main never lends a child the upstream's newer commits (it once showed every pushed child as "200 files · +40520 −425").
- `ompdesk_merge` merges into the parent checkout's current branch with `git merge --no-ff` (never a push, no remote command in the module). It refuses a detached parent, uncommitted work in child or parent, and a branch with nothing to merge (`mergeProblem`); asks one `[OMPdesk]` card only while `agentControlManage` (default on) is off; refuses a merge `predictMerge` says would conflict before running `git merge` (`MergeOutcome.predicted`, the files named, the parent untouched), and still aborts a conflict it did not predict (`git merge --abort`) naming the files.
- **The work board has no review section** (the user's decision): the agent that opened a child reviews and lands it itself with `ompdesk_compare`, `ompdesk_merge` and `ompdesk_session_stop`; nobody clicks Approve, Discard or Dismiss on a board (desktop or phone strip), and `agent_control_review` only lists. `AgentControl.reviewItems` stays: pushed as `AGENT_CONTROL_REVIEW` after a 400 ms settle on child activity and asked once on mount via the `agent_control_review` command `dispatchRpcCommand` answers itself, it feeds the "Child sessions" rows (title, status, step, clock, report, waiting) and the opener's next-prompt children. A child that needs the person (a question, a blocker, a plan waiting for approval: `AgentReviewItem.waiting`) shows as a waiting row and a notification. No item when nothing is left: idle (no turn, no open ask), branch landed, no uncommitted or untracked file, not failed, not waiting on an answer (its opened row reads merged). A landed branch with leftovers lists only those.
  - The review list follows the world, not only tab activity: besides the pushes after a child's activity and after `ompdesk_session_open`/`_merge`/`_stop`/`_autopilot`, `AgentControlRuntime.#sweepAll` (the 10 s timer that also runs the auto-close pass; `OMP_GUI_TEST_AGENT_SWEEP_MS` in e2e) re-reads each opener's `reviewItems` and sends `AGENT_CONTROL_REVIEW` only when the list differs from the one last sent (`#reviewSent`), so a commit, push or merge made outside the app moves a child's row without tab activity: an idle child whose branch has landed (an untouched branch counts: its tip is in the parent's HEAD) and has no uncommitted or untracked file has no item. `e2e/agent-review.e2e.ts` commits into each child's checkout from outside the app and waits for the board to list all three as opened rows, with no review section or button; `host.test.ts` pins the timer push (changed → sent, unchanged → silent).
  - A child's release always reaches the board that listed it. A child restored by a relaunch has its opener only in the pool (`openedBySession`), which forgets it with the tab, so its `released` found no opener and the board kept the stale item beside the closed record: one finished child read "2 done" (`e2e/opened-close-relaunch.e2e.ts`, 7 of 8 runs). `#listedIn` remembers, per child tab, the opener whose list (a push or the board's own read) last named it, and a release with no opener left pushes that opener's list. Pinned by "tells the opener's board when a child restored by a relaunch closes" in `host.test.ts`.
- Item: branch → base, files and +/−, uncommitted, the answer's start; `ompdesk_compare` reads the branch itself. The board shows none of it as buttons. A conflicting `ompdesk_merge` aborts the merge, names the files and keeps the child open. `ompdesk_session_stop` `close` closes the child's tab; `delete` deletes its session and, for a worktree child the caller opened, then removes its worktree and branch through `removeSafeWorktree` (`branches.ts`: a registered linked worktree only, clean incl. untracked, tip in the parent's HEAD or upstream, an untouched branch included; `worktree remove` without `--force`, `branch -d`). Anything unlanded keeps both, and the result names the path, branch and what is unlanded (`#dropWorktree`; pinned by `branches.test.ts` and `service.test.ts`).
- **Auto-close** (`AgentControl.autoClose`, `auto-close.ts` `autoCloseDecision`, run by `AgentControlRuntime` 1 s after tab activity, after a merge and every 10 s (`sweepMs`; `OMP_GUI_TEST_AGENT_SWEEP_MS` in e2e) because a merge or push outside the app moves no tab). An opened session (not the person's own, not an Autopilot child or run) closes its TAB (the session file stays; `AgentControlViewEvent.auto` → the renderer's `closeTab`, which refuses a tab on screen or with a composer draft: `lib/agent-auto-close.ts`) once it is idle with a final answer, nothing running (`isSettled`), ends with `Result: ok`, and has no branch or a landed branch with a clean worktree.
  - `parseWorkerResult` (`shared/agent-control.ts`) reads the last line only, never prose. `problem` keeps the session open as a problem row (review item `failed`, ✕). A missing line leaves it done but open, unless its transcript holds an `ompdesk_report` of kind `done` to its opener since the last prompt it was given (`transcriptEnding` in `service.ts`; the tool or its `xd://ompdesk_report` device, not refused): that report stands for the verdict.
  - **Across a relaunch** (v0.44.0 left two finished children open for hours: the opener a launch saw lived only in `#parents`, so a child the 0.43.0 relaunch restored was never closable). `host.ts` `#openerOf` reads the opener the pool kept (`openedBy`, else the tab holding `openedBySession`) for closability, the sweep timer, activity sweeps and `noteUserPrompt`; an Autopilot run is excluded through `isAutopilotRun` (`Autopilot.runRootOf`). Such a child is `ControlTab.restored`: its transcript says how it ended (final answer, `stopReason`, a person's own prompt keeps it open), read from its live omp, or from the saved file on the computer it runs on while the tab is deferred (`asleep`, `storedMessages`). An asleep child is not `running` in the review list. Pinned by `host.test.ts` ("restored after a relaunch … pushed straight to main"), the transcript cases of `service.test.ts` and `e2e/opened-close-relaunch.e2e.ts`.
  - Landed (`landingFacts`): tip in the parent's HEAD or upstream AND a commit of its own (an untouched branch has nothing to land); uncommitted or untracked files keep it open, reason `uncommitted`.
  - Never: a tab the person looks at (`active`/`visible`/viewer), one with a draft (`SidecarPool.hasDraft`) or a prompt of their own (`noteUserPrompt` in `dispatchRpcCommand`; agent messages are wrapped), a failed ending, unmerged commits, files left in the worktree, and not before the opener read the answer (`result`/`read`/`compare`/`merge`) unless the opener is idle.
  - The worktree goes with the close (`removeSafeWorktree`: `worktree remove` and `branch -d`, never `--force` or `-D`; re-checks landed and clean). Recorded in pref `agentClosedChildren`.
- Tests: `auto-close.test.ts`, `branches.test.ts` (real temp repos; bare remote with a stale parent main: a pushed child is landed with no diff, an unpushed one is one commit, a landed one with leftovers shows only them), `service.test.ts` (autoClose, `reviewItems`), `WorkBoardReview.test.tsx` (no review section or button on the board), `e2e/agent-review.e2e.ts` (real git: three worktrees, compare, merge two through the tool with no card by default, close a third; the board lists only opened rows), `e2e/family-opened-close.e2e.ts` (fake omp with `OMP_GUI_TEST_SESSION_FILES=1`: the plain and the merged session close and fold into "2 done", the unmerged one stays a failed row; the merged worktree and branch are removed, the other kept).
- Opt-in real omp (`OMP_GUI_REAL_WORK=1`): the third test of `e2e/real-agent-control.e2e.ts` opens a worktree child that commits a file, compares, merges (no card by default; the test approves one if it shows), creates and lists a schedule. Stock presents host tools as `xd://` devices, so the journal shows `write` calls to `xd://ompdesk_*`.

### Beyond the caller's window
- `AgentControlHost.tabs` returns every open tab of the app, the caller's window first (`ControlTab.here`, `device`, `sessionPath`; `SidecarPool.tabWindows`/`windowOfTab`); every call to a tab goes through the window that owns it, so send, wait, answer, show and stop reach other windows and a paired device's tabs (its host's grants and `PeerQuota` apply).
- `ompdesk_sessions` takes `scope` `window` (default) | `app` | `stored`, `device` (a paired host by name or id, `local`), `folder`, `status` (`running` | `waiting` | `idle`), `mine`, `query`, `limit`, and always ends with the paired devices and their state (`AgentControlHostDeps.remote.hosts`).
- `stored` lists the session index of this computer (`SessionIndex.list`/`searchContent`) or a paired host (`sessions.list`/`sessions.search`). Rows are cached by stock id (`#stored`), so `_session_read`, `reopen`, `_session_stop` and `ompdesk_compare` take a saved id (an open saved session goes through its tab). A row's `tabId` is only what was true when it was listed: every use asks `#liveTab` (that tab if still open, else a tab whose `sessionPath` and `device` are the row's), so a tab the device closed since never makes a reopen say "already open" (the device's `tab.close` releases the host's tab at once; only a dropped link waits out the grace). `ompdesk_session_stop` takes a saved id with optional `device` (looked up on that device when not cached): with no tab holding it there is nothing to stop or close, and `delete` asks one card unless full control is on, then deletes the file on the device it is saved on (`deleteStored` → the sidebar's delete, a host's `sessions.delete`). A saved transcript is read through stock `loadSessionMessagesReadOnly` (no writer, no lock: `sessions.messages`, `StockSessionStore.messages`, `SessionIndex.readMessages`) or the host's `sessions.messages` (`LocalHostServices`; refuses a path the host's user hid, dep `agentHidden`, code `session_hidden`), paged from the end with an index cursor.
- `ompdesk_session_open` takes `device` (spawned through `spawnTab`; folder absolute or that device's workspace; a `worktree` is made on that device, through the caller's own tab when the caller runs there, else `host.git.worktreeCreate`; an offline device refuses) and `reopen` (a saved session's file on the device holding it; never with folder/worktree/mode, never marked a worker, refused only while a live tab holds it).
- **A stored session opens in the folder its file records** (stock's header cwd, `SessionIndex.recordedCwd`), whoever opens it: `spawnTabForWindow` for this computer's windows and agents, `LocalHostServices` `tab.open` for a paired device, never the caller's or the app's folder (v0.46.0 reopened a worktree child in the user's main checkout). A failed read of that folder fails the open (never "none recorded", which would fall back to the caller's folder). A recorded folder that is gone is refused (`folder-missing` with `folder`; `session_folder_missing` on a host), never opened elsewhere: stock would relocate a removed worktree's session into the launch folder, the repository's main checkout. Chats keep the Work workspace.
  - **Restore**: when disk care saved that folder (`savedWorktreesForSpawn.find`, a `SavedWorktree` whose `path` is the folder) the refusal carries `saved` and the window's toast offers Restore (`openTab` again with `restoreSaved`: `sessionFolderState` in `tab-spawn.ts` runs `restoreSavedWorktree`, then the session opens there); without a record the toast says the folder is gone, nothing was saved and the session stays listed. An agent's reopen is told the saved worktree and `ompdesk_session_open {"reopen": id, "restore": true}`. **A paired host's session follows the same rule on the host**: the device asks `sessions.folder {sessionPath, restore}` (`LocalHostServices`, the same `sessionFolderState` over the host's index and saved worktrees) before it opens the tab, so the refusal, the offer and the restore are the host's; a restore gets `WORKTREE_PREPARE_BUDGET_MS` (seeding and setup hook) and a failed or timed-out restore opens nothing; a host too old to answer a plain lookup still refuses a gone folder in its own `tab.open`. Listings say it before the click (`useProjectNames(cwd).gone`): the sidebar hides such rows, the session picker shows "Folder removed" and opens the row as a tab (stock refuses an in-place switch to another folder), the Work tree and the work board's closed children show it on the row. Memory pages link no sessions to open.
  - Pinned by `tab-spawn.test.ts` (local, paired host, a held and a timed-out restore), `host-services.test.ts` (`sessions.folder` restoring a real worktree), `host.test.ts`, `stores/tabs.test.tsx`, `SessionPickerDialog.test.tsx` and three tests of `e2e/agent-control-across.e2e.ts` (a reopen in its own folder, then refused once the folder is gone; a saved worktree restored by `restore: true` on this computer and on a paired device).
- `ompdesk_compare` reads per session the final answer, the check-like commands its transcript ran with outcome and the last `CHECK_TAIL_CHARS` (1 200) of their output (`checksRun`), a worktree's branch facts and `branchEvidence` (the unified diff against the same base `branchFacts` measures from, cut fairly across files by `boundDiff`: each file an equal share, unused shares go to the larger ones, a cut file ends with `… N more lines of <path> left out`; plus the merge prediction). Both git reads run on the computer the session runs on (`host.git.facts`, `host.git.evidence {worktree, diffChars}`). With `criteria` (non-empty strings; no count limit) main builds one judge prompt (`JUDGE_EVIDENCE_CHARS` 60 000 characters of evidence split per session: facts whole, answer ≤ 20 %, the newest `JUDGE_CHECKS` (4) check tails ≤ 15 %, the diff the rest) and sends `agent_control_judge` to the CALLER's omp (local handler → bridge `agent_control.judge` → `completeOnSmol` over `JUDGE_ROLES` advisor then slow (`agent-control-judge-core.ts`): the first credentialed candidate whose `Model.identity.class` differs from the session model's, else the first credentialed one; 170 s deadline; purpose `ompdesk-judge` journaled under the role that served it; a model command in `quota-commands.ts`). The verdict line names the model, its role and family against the caller's (`Verdict by openai-codex/gpt-6.1-sol (advisor role, openai family; your model is anthropic)`, or `same family as your model: …`). `parseVerdict` (zod) yields a ranked verdict, `yes`/`partly`/`no`/`unknown` per criterion and a winner; a reply that is not the JSON is shown as is; a judge that fails (no credentials, timeout, provider error) leaves `Verdict: unavailable (<reason>)` above the full evidence, never a failed compare.
- A saved row or untitled tab reads as its task (`storedTitle`, `agentTaskTitle`) or `(untitled, in <folder>)`, never the raw agent-message wrapper; an opened tab's `ControlTab.title` falls back to `IpcTabInfo.task`. A tab with neither (restored by a relaunch, which keeps only the stock title the layout saved; reopened) is named once per session by `#nameUntitled` (`service.ts`, run before every tool call and review): the session index row of the computer it runs on, its title else `agentTaskTitle` of its first message, handed to the pool as the tab's `task` so the sidebar and board read it too (v0.45.0's relaunch listed every child "(untitled, in gui-agent-…)").
- **An id an agent was given survives a relaunch** (v0.45.0: every send and stop on a child's old id failed "No session … open in this app" while the same session was open again). Each tab's descriptor in the saved layout carries `formerIds` (its id at save time, then the ids it had before, newest first, ≤ `MAX_FORMER_TAB_IDS` 8; `tab-layout.ts`), restored as `PoolEntry.former` with the session file they named; `SidecarPool.formerTabIds` answers them only while the tab still holds that file, and `ControlTab.formerIds` lets `#find` resolve an exact former id after the current ids (a prefix matches current ids only). Pinned by the "after a relaunch" cases of `service.test.ts`, `sidecar-pool.test.ts` ("earlier tab ids"), `tab-layout.test.ts` and `e2e/relaunch-child-ids.e2e.ts`.
- **One way to name a session, every tool** (20 Oct 2026, the user's rule: the main agent handles the control tools by itself in every situation). `#find` (`service.ts`) resolves for every tool alike, first match wins: a tab id, an id it had before a relaunch (whole), the saved (stock) id of the session a tab holds (`_<id>.jsonl` of its file, or a stored row the last list printed), a unique prefix of a tab id or of that saved id, the title or task exactly or by a unique start (any case; never a hidden session's), then a saved session no tab holds (`#storedSession`: the listed rows, this computer's index, then every paired device's, so a saved id works after a relaunch forgot the lists; devices that could not be searched are named). A name that matches several fails with the candidates. A tool that needs an open tab (send, answer, focus, merge, report, autopilot) given a saved-only session says so with the exact `ompdesk_session_open {"reopen": …}`; `ompdesk_session_result` reads a saved-only session's last answer from its file.
- **A paired device's blip never fails a call.** `#reach` waits up to `LINK_WAIT_MS` (15 s: the client reconnects after 1 s, then dials up to 5 s per address) while a named device is connecting or offline, before a stored list or read, an open or reopen there, a schedule or Autopilot start there; `#reachTab` does the same for a device's tab (its `error` status while the link is down, then the re-attach's `starting`, within `REATTACH_WAIT_MS` 10 s), so read, send, answer, stop and the rest act on it once it can answer; `#overLink` makes a read (a transcript page, a saved transcript, a stored index) cut by a drop once more after the link is back, never a prompt or an answer. A device that stays away fails within 20 s saying to check it is on and on the tailnet. A listing names such a tab's state as its device's link being down, never "stopped (error)".
- **Stop and close never hang on stock's abort**, which answers only once the turn unwound (v0.45.0: two closes failed "RPC timeout (8000ms): abort"). A close sends the abort and gives it `ABORT_BEFORE_CLOSE_MS` (3 s), then closes anyway (closing ends the omp), and confirms the window dropped the tab within `CLOSE_WAIT_MS` (a window's last tab or one with unsaved Files edits stays: the answer says to ask the user). A plain stop waits up to `STOP_WAIT_MS` (30 s) and, unconfirmed, answers what it saw and the next call (result, or close); a delete waits the same for the turn to end and retries main's `session_running` refusal until then.
- **A close waits for the omp's exit, not only for the tab** (`#whenGone`: a close, an auto-close, a failed open's cleanup). A closed tab leaves the lists before its omp process is gone, and on Windows that process keeps the folder it works in from being removed: measured in `e2e/agent-control-across.e2e.ts` (4 of 4 runs failed `EBUSY rmdir …\elsewhere`), the tab was gone 139 ms after the close and its `cmd.exe` shim and `bun` (cwd = the session's folder) lived another 10–45 ms, so the reply "Closed", the next `rm` of the folder and an auto-close's `git worktree remove` all ran while it still worked there. After the window drops the tab, `#whenGone` waits `OMP_EXIT_WAIT_MS` (5 s) for `AgentControlHost.exited` (`SidecarPool.whenTabExited`: the exit `#releaseEntry` already holds for `onTabReleased`; a paired device's tab has none). Pinned by `service.test.ts` (close, auto-close removal and a failed open's discard each wait for it) and `sidecar-pool.test.ts`.
- **A card is answered with whatever field fits it**: `choice` or `text` for a one-question ask, `text` or `choice` for a text prompt, `approve`, `choice` or `text` (yes/no) for a confirmation; `approve` names no option of a plain choice card. A field that cannot answer the card fails naming the card and the field that does (several questions list every question id and its options).
- The situation matrix (`service.test.ts` "situation matrix", "answer matrix"; two-app link blip in `e2e/agent-control-across.e2e.ts`) runs every tool against a local tab, a paired device's tab, a deferred tab, a saved-only session, a busy session, a reconnecting device and a hidden session, by each kind of name. A new tool or situation adds its cells there.
- Tests: `service.test.ts` (scopes, filters, stored list/read/paging, device open, reopen and stop of a saved row after its tab closed, judge, `checksRun`, `parseVerdict`), `host.test.ts` (another window, device spawn rules, stored rows local and remote), `src/main/remote/host-services.test.ts` (`sessions.messages`, hidden refused), `e2e/agent-control-across.e2e.ts` (another window, a judged compare, a closed session found/read/reopened; two paired apps: a session opened on the device, its prompt in the host's record, its transcript read back and paged, reopened after its tab closed and deleted by saved id and device; a device session shown, notifying and reporting to its local opener; a schedule and a worktree session on the device), `e2e/agent-control-remote.e2e.ts` (Autopilot and `ompdesk_app` on the device), opt-in fourth test of `e2e/real-agent-control.e2e.ts`.

### Mailbox (`ompdesk_report`)
- A session another session opened reports with `ompdesk_report` (`kind` progress | done | blocked | question | review | note, `text`, `files`, `checks`, `to` a sibling, `dispatch`, `id`). `mail.ts` holds the rules (`Mailbox`, `deliveryFor`, `mailText`); `service.ts` `#report` routes: the opener by `AgentControlHost.parentOf` (this launch's `#parents`, else `IpcTabInfo.openedBy`, else the tab holding `openedBySession`); a sibling only under the same opener, written as `note`; any session to one working in the same checkout or repository (`AgentControlHost.checkoutPeers`, see Sessions sharing a checkout), question/blocked kept, every other kind written as `note`; nobody else. Decision gates go to the parent and the person and are never auto-resolved.
- Delivery is ONE stock custom message in the receiver (`agent_control_mail` → bridge `agent_control.mail` → `pi.sendMessage`, customType `ompdesk.mail` from `resources/gui-bridge/agent-control-contracts.ts`): question/blocked/done/review `aside` + `triggerTurn` (an idle receiver starts a turn, except in plan mode or after the person's Stop, where stock folds it into context); progress/note no turn (`triggerTurn: false`, shown at once when idle). Progress from one sender is coalesced for `MAIL_COALESCE_MS` (2 s, latest wins, `merged` counts the rest); any other report supersedes pending progress.
- **Every report reaches a busy receiver during its turn** (the user's report, 21 Oct 2026: an orchestrator in a long `wait` on a background `gh run watch` showed four child reports as "이 턴이 끝나면 보여요" rows; done/review were `followUp`, read only after the turn, and the bridge held progress/note until it ended). Stock's `aside` (omp 18.12.5 `agent-session.ts`) is queued and injected at the next step boundary without cutting the tool batch (a `steer` skips the batch's remaining calls) and ends an interruptible `wait`; one stranded after the loop's last poll wakes a turn once idle (`#resumeStrandedIrcAsides`). So done/review go as `aside`, and the bridge sends a no-turn report as `aside` while `isStreaming` (idle it keeps the plain delivery: an idle aside starts a turn); it also turns an older paired main's `followUp` into an aside. Never `nextTurn` (19 Oct 2026: hidden and unjournaled until the person's next prompt, so notes appeared half an hour late) and never stock's default no-turn delivery during a turn (it steers). **Cost**: a note costs nothing when it lands mid-batch (it rides the request that carries the batch's results), and one more model request when it lands while the turn's last reply streams (stock's stop boundary drains asides into the same run) or after the loop's last poll (a woken turn); coalescing bounds progress to one such request per 2 s per sender. Pinned by `bridge-agent-mail-stock.test.ts` (installed omp, scripted loopback model: two parallel `sleep 3` calls both run and the request that carries their results carries the done and the note, 3 requests in all; a `wait` on a `sleep 300` background job returns on a done; a note during the last reply costs exactly one request; an idle receiver takes a turn on a done and none on a progress), which fails on the previous delivery.
- The model reads `<ompdesk-mail from=… kind=… dispatch=…>` plus a one-line instruction (answer a question with `ompdesk_session_send`; a user-only choice goes to the user, never guessed). The transcript draws `MailNote` (`CustomMessageCard.tsx`, `data-agent-mail`, display only): one quiet line `⑂ Session report · <title> · <kind>` whose chevron button (`aria-expanded`; Enter/Space; the global touch reach) opens the body, the text, changed files and checks (`data-agent-mail-body`, not in the DOM while closed). A question or blocker starts open with its warning tint (it waits on a decision); done, review, progress and note start closed. The person's toggle is kept per report id in memory and `sessionStorage` (`lib/mail-open.ts`, newest 300). Pinned by `MailNote.test.tsx`, `CustomMessageCard.test.tsx`; captures `OMP_GUI_MAIL_COLLAPSE=<dir>` (`e2e/mail-collapse.e2e.ts`, harness `?mail=1`). The work board's opened rows show the last report (`AgentReviewItem.report`; `waiting` puts a child with an unanswered question first; `opened-children.ts`).
- Dispatch ids: every message an opener sends its child carries `[ompdesk dispatch dN: …]` (and `[ompdesk request <id>]` for a `request_id`); a new dispatch marks the child's question answered, and a report naming an older dispatch is ignored (`stale`).
- The journals are the record (the one OMPdesk store is the outbox below). A report's id defaults to a hash of both session files, kind, dispatch and text, so a call stock re-runs after a relaunch has the same id; the receiver's bridge drops an id its branch already holds (`hasMailEntry` over stock's `custom_message` entries) or this process already handed to stock, and answers `{duplicate: true, journaled}` (`journaled` false = only queued by this omp). A report counts as delivered (`#seen`) only after delivery succeeded; a failed one is retried by the sender with the same id (progress `MAIL_PROGRESS_RETRIES` = 3). After a relaunch main reads dispatch and request ids back from the child's transcript (`journalMarks`), so fencing and `request_id` idempotency of `ompdesk_session_send` hold. Concurrent sends with one `request_id` (keyed by both session files) join one in-flight delivery.
- **A report survives its receiver's omp ending** (21 Oct 2026: a child's `done`, then a `followUp`, to a parent inside a long tool call vanished across an update restart). Measured on the installed omp (`bridge-agent-mail-restart-stock.test.ts`): an aside queued during a model request or a tool call is journaled when a quit disposes the session (stdin closed the way `SidecarManager` closes an omp), for every kind, but a SIGKILL before the next step boundary (a crash, a force-quit of OMPdesk) loses it. So `AgentControlRuntime` keeps every accepted report in `MailOutbox` (`mail-outbox.ts`, `<userData>/mail-outbox.json`, atomic write at accept time and never in a quit hook, newest `MAIL_OUTBOX_MAX` = 100, none older than `MAIL_OUTBOX_TTL_MS` = 3 days; an earlier build's `followUp` entry is read as an aside) per receiver session file and paired device, unless the first answer already says journaled. On the sweep timer (`sweepMs`, 10 s) `redeliverMail` sends each entry again, with its id, to the tab that holds the receiver's session now (`SidecarPool.sessionOwner`; a relaunch gives it a new tab id; a closed tab waits until the session is reopened): the bridge delivers an id it never saw (the omp that replaced the killed one), answers `{duplicate, journaled: false}` for one it still queues (left alone), and `{duplicate, journaled: true}` settles the entry. Never delivered twice: the receiver's journal and `queuedMail` dedupe by id. A report journaled but never answered by a turn counts as delivered: the model reads it with the journal when the interrupted turn continues.
- Receipts: a send answers `accepted` plus `turn started` when the target is seen running within `TURN_START_WAIT_MS` (1.5 s). No count or rate cap; only a self-send is refused.
- **A child's question card tells its opener** (20 Oct 2026: a child opened on a paired Windows device raised a stock `ask`, and its opener learned of it only by polling `ompdesk_session_result`; a local child's card woke nobody either). The pool's `ask` activity (`SidecarPool.#announceAsk`, from a local omp's frame or a paired host's `extensionUi`/`openAsks` alike) reaches `AgentControlRuntime`, which calls `AgentControl.childAsked(opener, child, requestId)` for a session the opener opened: one `question` mail (`aside`, wakes an idle opener) whose text is the card (`cardText`: title, options as written, the `ompdesk_session_answer` field that answers it) and `Mail.card` the request id, so `mailText` points at `ompdesk_session_answer` instead of `ompdesk_session_send`. Id `ask-<child>-<request>`, told once per card (`#cardsTold`; the bridge drops a repeat by id). Never OMPdesk's own `[OMPdesk]` card, a session hidden from agents, or an Autopilot run's children (its approver answers them). While the card is open the child's review item is `waiting` (board row, sidebar, next-prompt children). `ompdesk_session_answer` on a choice card with no `choice` says which field to pass (`No choice given: pass \`choice\` …`, and how to type an answer of one's own through `Other (type your own)`) instead of calling the value wrong. Pinned by the question-card cases of `service.test.ts` and `host.test.ts` and the question-card test of `e2e/agent-control-across.e2e.ts` (two apps).
- **A queued follow-up is never invisible** (v0.45.0: children reported done or asked, then waited in the same turn while the opener's follow-up sat in stock's queue, which the model never sees, and said "d5/d6 has not arrived"). A send to a session that reported done, review, question or blocked to the caller since its current turn started (`#reportedAt`, against `ControlTab.busySince`) goes as `steer` whatever `delivery` says, and the receipt says why. A follow-up that is queued behind a busy child's turn is remembered (`#queuedFollowUp`: its dispatch); when that child reports before its transcript holds the dispatch (`journalMarks`), the report's answer says the message waits and to end the turn now (`#queuedNote`, also on a stale or repeated report). Pinned by the "after a relaunch" cases of `service.test.ts` and the busy-child test of `e2e/relaunch-child-ids.e2e.ts`.
- Tests: `service.test.ts` (delivery per kind, question waiting, stale dispatch, repeated id, coalescing, busy receiver, siblings, receipts, retried failure, fencing and request ids after a relaunch), `bridge-agent-mail.test.ts` (stock `sendMessage`, journal and process dedupe, aside while a turn runs, plain when idle, an older main's `followUp`), `bridge-agent-mail-stock.test.ts`, `bridge-agent-mail-restart-stock.test.ts` (per kind what a quit journals; a done SIGKILL-cut during a tool call reaches the relaunched omp's journal exactly once through the outbox), `mail-outbox.test.ts` (keep, settle, bounds, age, damaged file, an earlier build's entry, paired-device lookup, skipped receivers), the "busy parent ... replaces it" case of `host.test.ts`, `CustomMessageCard.test.tsx`, `opened-children.test.ts`, `e2e/agent-mail.e2e.ts` (two children report and finish, one asks and is answered, the parent's turns start on done and question with no polling tool; a progress report lands at once for an idle parent, and a progress and a done land during a busy parent's turn, with no prompt; a report repeated after a relaunch arrives once).

### Sessions sharing a checkout
- The user's rule (20 Oct 2026): sessions working in the same project folder at the same time know of each other and talk instead of breaking each other's work. Measured first (stock omp, two `omp -p` sessions on the smol role, no OMPdesk): in 3 of 9 same-checkout runs one session's work landed in the other's commit (a plain `git commit` after the other's `git add`, a `git add -A`, a formatter plus `add -A`); `checkout --`/`restore`/`reset --hard`/`clean` never came up; a shared formatter rewrote the other session's files every time; stash was used by 5 of 6 sessions in two worktrees of one repository with no swap in 3 runs (the swap is real, it happened that day). Stock has no cross-process channel to reuse: `src/irc` is one process's bus (subagents), `collab` shares one session with browsers. With the guard (real omp in OMPdesk, two tabs, 3 runs each of guard alone and guard plus messaging): separate files 0 of 6 wrong commits (8 refusals, 3 `add -A` and 5 commits without paths, each redone by name); the same files, guard alone ended right 1 of 3 (the refusal said "ask the user", nobody answered, one lost change), guard plus messaging 6 of 6 in about 40 s, one conflict settled by a question and its answer; what remained was each session overwriting the shared file's uncommitted lines with `echo >`/heredocs, hence the overwrite rule below.
- Two scopes, as git keeps them: the worktree (`git rev-parse --show-toplevel`: files, index, HEAD, build output) and the repository (`--git-common-dir`: stash, branches, refs, shared by every worktree). Main's `CheckoutPeerCoordinator` (`src/main/checkout-peers.ts`, wired in `ipc.ts`) groups this computer's live omps (the tabs of its windows and `LocalHostServices.checkoutMembers()`, the tabs it runs for paired devices; a paired host's tab is that host's to coordinate) by both, on tab activity and every `CHECKOUT_PEERS_SWEEP_MS` (10 s; `OMP_GUI_TEST_CHECKOUT_PEERS_MS`), reads each same-worktree member's changed files from its bridge (`checkout_touched`) and pushes each member `checkout_peers_set` (`CheckoutPeers`, `resources/gui-bridge/modes/checkout-peers-contracts.ts`) only on a change; a session never told of company starts alone and one left alone is told once. A session hidden from agents is a peer with no id, title or files; one run for a paired device has no id (its control tools answer on the device).
- The bridge (`modes/checkout-peers.ts`, rules `checkout-peers-core.ts`, capability `checkoutPeers`) adds the `<shared-checkout>` section through `registerPromptSection` (names and ids of both groups, stage and commit by name, change a shared file with the edit tool, formatters on own files only, one `ompdesk_report` note before a commit, install, rebuild or dev-server restart, a question to have a peer commit or hold); it carries no files, so it stays the same text turn after turn and the prompt cache holds. A `tool_call` guard reads `bash` with Autopilot's own parser (`shellEffects` in `autopilot-guard-core.ts`, a collect mode of its walk: git runs and output redirects) and judges each git run by one table (`GIT_RULES`): stash (not list/show) and deleting, moving or force-setting a branch while any session works in any worktree of the repository; `commit -a`, a commit without paths (the index is shared), `checkout`/`switch` of a branch, `reset --hard` or a reset without paths, `clean -f` while one works in the same worktree; `add`, `commit --`, `checkout --`, `restore`, `reset --`, `rm`, `mv` of a path that is a glob, pathspec magic, the top level or above, or holds a file a peer changed. Writing a file a peer changed whole (a shell `>`/`>>`, the `write` tool: `writeRefusal`) is refused with the edit tool as the way (it applies only its own lines and refuses an edit whose lines moved). A git run in another repository is not judged; a session alone is never judged. Paths are compared in main's spelling: the bridge resolves its cwd, its writes and each git run's cwd to the native real path of the nearest existing folder (`canonicalPath`; macOS `/var` is `/private/var`, Windows temp dirs have 8.3 names). The refusal names the command, why, each peer (id, title, files changed lately), the files this session changed and the exact `ompdesk_report {"to": …, "kind": "question"}` to agree who goes first (or "ask the user" without the control tools). A session's own files are what its file tools (`fileToolPaths`) and its shell redirects (`shellWrites`, devices left out) wrote, recorded whether or not it has company yet.
- `ompdesk_report {to: <peer>}` works from any session (no opener needed): a question or blocker reaches a busy peer between its steps and wakes an idle one; every other kind is a note that reaches a busy peer between its steps and never wakes it. Hidden peers refuse it like every control tool.
- The work board's "Same checkout" row reads the peers back: `CheckoutPeersState` (`checkout-peers-core.ts`) keeps what `checkout_peers_set` carried, and the bridge emits a `checkout_peers` frame (`{peers: CheckoutPeers | null}`, `CHECKOUT_PEERS_EVENT`) only when it changed; `checkout_peers.get` → main `get_checkout_peers` (free in `quota-commands.ts`) hydrates it like `get_goal`, into the session store's `checkoutPeers` (a paired host's tab carries its host's frame). The row (`work-board/WorkBoardSession.tsx`) counts the worktree's peers, else the repository's, and never shows on a session alone; a hidden peer counts and reads "a hidden session". Test: `bridge-checkout-peers-frame.test.ts`.
- Tests: `bridge-checkout-peers.test.ts` (guard and section; the cross-worktree stash case first; a cwd through a symlink; shell writes counted as the session's own), `checkout-peers.test.ts` (grouping on real temp repositories), the peer case of `service.test.ts`, `e2e/shared-checkout.e2e.ts` (two tabs in one checkout: the second writes its file from the shell; the first agent's whole-file write and shell overwrite of that file and its `git add -A` are refused, it commits its own file by name, the second's edit stays unstaged and uncommitted, a note and a question reach the second; the fake plays turns through the real guard, `fixture checkout:` in `e2e/fixture/checkout-peers.ts`), opt-in `e2e/real-shared-checkout.e2e.ts` (`OMP_GUI_REAL_WORK=1`: two real sessions, guard alone vs guard plus messaging, on separate and on the same files; results appended to `/tmp/shared-checkout-measure/real-runs.jsonl`).

### Schedules and Autopilot
- `ompdesk_schedule` `create`/`list`/`cancel` (`service.ts`; `AgentControlHost.schedules(device?)` in `host.ts`: no device → this computer's `ScheduleService` via dep `schedules` ← `IpcDeps.schedules` ← `scheduleService` in `index.ts`; a paired host → `host.schedules.*` over the link, served by that host's own `ScheduleService`) goes through `command({action:"get"|"delete"})` and `create(draft)`, so the Schedules page, its log and its one-run-per-schedule rule apply on the computer that runs it. `create` (`host.schedules.create` on a host) answers the id its own save made (`ScheduleCreated`): never a diff against an earlier read, which handed one of two concurrent creates the other's schedule.
  - `device` (name or id, `"local"` = this computer) picks the computer; default the device the caller's session runs on, so a session on a paired host schedules on that host. A device whose link is down is waited for (`#reach`, see Beyond the caller's window), then refused; a create on another device than the caller's needs `folder` (the caller's folder is not a path there).
  - A create raises one `[OMPdesk]` card only while `agentControlManage` (default on) is off; names the schedule `[agent] <name>` (`AGENT_SCHEDULE_PREFIX`; main-owned provenance shown in that computer's Settings › Schedules; only such schedules can be cancelled by an agent); never sets `autoApprove` (a print-mode run cannot ask, so tools needing approval are denied); leaves the interval to the service's own minimum; refuses a relative folder. These agent rules live in the caller's `service.ts`, the one place that knows an agent asked; the host checks the request's shape (`SchedulesCommandParams`, `SchedulesCreateParams`) and its service the draft.
  - `SCHEDULE_RUNS_KEPT` 200 (`shared/schedules.ts`) bounds the service's run log, and a start while the previous run goes is recorded as skipped; neither limits how many schedules an agent creates.
  - Tests: `service.test.ts`, `host.test.ts` (local and remote source), `remote/host-services.test.ts` (`host.schedules.*`), `e2e/agent-control-across.e2e.ts` "on a paired device" (create, list and cancel on the host; the host's own `schedules.json`; the device-routed Settings read and the host's push).
- `ompdesk_autopilot` (`action` start | status | stop; `AutopilotSource` in `service.ts`, adapted by `host.ts` over `Autopilot.handleCommand` through the optional dep `autopilot`, wired in `ipc.ts`; skill reference `references/autopilot.md`): a start goes through the one `#start` path from a tab of the folder's computer that has the folder open (default the caller's own folder on the caller's own device; `device` names another, `local` = this computer). On this computer the folder must be open in a tab of the caller's window; on a paired host a session is opened there to start from when none has it and closed once the run started. `ship` commit/push/release maps to the sheet presets and is always sent (omitted: commit), so an agent's start always names a level and never gets Automatic, and the card says what it will do. `branch`/`advanced` map 1:1 onto `AutopilotStartOptions`. It raises one `[OMPdesk]` card only while `agentControlManage` (default on) is off, and always for ship release and for the default branch, `main` or `master`, whatever the switch says (a push to main is never loosened; push and release from Autopilot keep the card the ship level needs). The default branch comes from the folder's idle `get_autopilot` on the computer that holds it (`plan` in `host.ts`, local and paired alike). No cap on agent-started runs. `stop` = End, `abort` = Stop now (pauses).
  - **A paired host's folder is the host's run** (19 Oct 2026, the user's rule: every agent tool works on a device's sessions as on local ones). `host.ts` sends `get_autopilot`/`set_autopilot` of a paired host's tab through its link (`dispatch`; a start gets `MAX_RPC_COMMAND_TIMEOUT_MS`), and the plan reads the host's default branch from its idle `get_autopilot`. On the host `LocalHostServices` answers both through its own coordinator, never the omp (`attachAutopilot`, `src/main/remote/hosted-autopilot.ts`; a start counts one of the device's requests in `PeerQuota`): the coordinator's pool reads the device's tab by its resume id as the source, and the run is a tab of the host's front window (`getTargetWindow`; see Autopilot › Overview, Ownership). So worktree, guard, ShipRunner, notifications and restart resume are the host's. The answer names the run's session file; the device opens it (`spawnTab {device, sessionPath}`), the host attaches that as a view of the run's tab (one omp), and the device tab counts as the caller's opened run, so status and stop go through it to the window tab that runs it. The run is charged to the device until it ends (`chargeRun` with its root and session; `ipc.ts` feeds every reply of a pool tab whose `Autopilot.runRootOf` names a charged run to `runEvents`; `runEnded` → `endRun`), whether or not the device still views it. Pinned by `service.test.ts`, `host.test.ts`, `host-services.test.ts`, `hosted-autopilot.test.ts`, `autopilot.test.ts` and `e2e/agent-control-remote.e2e.ts` (closing the device's view leaves the run going and its next cycle still counts).

### New worktree preparation
- `src/main/local/git/worktree-seed.ts`, run by `createWorktree` after `worktree add`, so the worktree dialog, Autopilot and `ompdesk_session_open` worktrees all get it (stock omp 18.12.1 has no `worktreeinclude` and no worktree hook).
- `.worktreeinclude` at the main checkout's root lists paths or globs (`*`, `?`, `**`; `#` comments) copied from the main checkout only when `git check-ignore` reports them ignored (a tracked file never is). Refused: absolute paths, `..`, `.git`, `!`, any match whose real parent leaves the checkout. A symlink inside a copied folder is recreated only when it points inside; **on Windows a file link is copied (a file symlink there needs Developer Mode or admin, and OMPdesk never requires one) and a folder link becomes a junction** (`worktree-seed.test.ts` "copies an ignored file link on Windows"; tests that make a file symlink use `itWithFileSymlinks` from `src/main/test-symlinks.ts`, skipped where the OS refuses). Budget `SEED_MAX_BYTES` 50 MB / `SEED_MAX_FILES` 10,000; existing files never overwritten; every skip reported (`WorktreeSeedReport.skipped`).
- Then `.ompdesk/worktree-setup` (`.cmd` on Windows), only when it exists in the new worktree or the main checkout, runs in the worktree under the setup supervisor (`/bin/sh <file>`, env `OMPDESK_ROOT_PATH`/`OMPDESK_WORKTREE_PATH`, `SETUP_TIMEOUT_MS` 120 s, process tree killed at the timeout and its exit code then ignored, output in `<worktree git dir>/ompdesk-worktree-setup.log`). A failure is reported in `RpcWorktreeCreateResult.setup`, never fatal.
- The dialog toasts both (`announcePreparation`, `worktree.seed*`/`worktree.setup*`); `ompdesk_session_open` adds `describePreparation` to its notes. `worktree_create`'s budget includes it (`WORKTREE_PREPARE_BUDGET_MS`; renderer, agent-control and Autopilot wait 960 s). Pinned by `worktree-seed.test.ts` (real temp repos).

### Worktree names
- Files: `shared/project-origin.ts` (rules), `main/local/git/origin.ts` (git reads), `renderer/lib/project-origin.ts` (`useProjectNames`), `stores/project-origins.ts`, channel `project-origin:get` = `window.omp.projectOrigins.get(cwds)`. A linked worktree's folder (`~/.omp/wt/gui-<name>-<hash7>`) never shows as a project name.
- Git alone says what it is: `--git-common-dir` names the main checkout (`<main>/.git`, else the first `git worktree list` entry for a bare or relocated dir); `--git-dir` ≠ common dir marks a linked worktree; the branch gives its kind (`autopilot/…` → "오토파일럿"/Autopilot, `omp/agent/…` → "병렬 작업"/Parallel work, or the child's title where a one-line `project · title` is shown; any other branch shows as itself).
- No new store: main caches each cwd for 30 s (5 s when git cannot place it); the renderer asks once per cwd and retries an unknown one after 10 s. A lookup that fails (no answer: the computer restarting, a dropped link) is no unknown: the folder keeps what was known and the store asks again by itself every 2 s until one answers (`stores/project-origins.ts` `RETRY_FAILED_MS`; a null would hide a worktree's rows as a removed folder until the folder list changed; `project-origins.test.ts`). A folder git cannot place (removed worktree, not a repository, a paired host's path) shows its own name with the `gui-…-<hash7>` dressing stripped.
- Surfaces: in the sidebar a linked worktree's sessions are **rows of their project's own group, never a group of their own** (the user's report, 21 Oct 2026: an Autopilot run showed as its own "⑂ 오토파일럿" group with its own +): in the project's order (pins, then recency), each marked at the row's end by what it is for (`data-sidebar-worktree`: `Rocket` for `autopilot`, `GitBranch` for `agent` and a user's own branch), the kind's word (`Autopilot`/`Parallel work`, else the branch) in the row's tooltip and screen-reader text. A project whose own folder has no session gets its group from its worktrees' sessions, keyed and named by the main checkout (its `+` starts there), never by the worktree. The group header and its `+` are the project's only; phone and tablet the same. Title bar and pane header breadcrumb `acme-web › Autopilot › <title>` (`data-titlebar-worktree`); work board head `acme-web · Autopilot` (`data-board-project`); window title `<name> — acme-web · Autopilot`; an untitled worktree tab chip reads from `IpcTabWorktree` (`baseCwd` + branch, no git); session and workspace pickers. **A new surface that shows a cwd's folder name goes through `useProjectNames`, never `basename`.**
- An Autopilot run's children (`autopilot/<run>-c<N>-<area>`, `autopilotRunOf`) are no rows of the project's list of their own: they sit right under the run's row, newest first, one step in and with no mark (`data-session-run-child`; `runParentOf` in `Sidebar.tsx`, from the branch git reports or, while git cannot place a new child worktree, the child tab's `worktree.baseCwd`). Parallel work sessions nest under their opener as family rows when the opener is listed (see Sidebar families), else are plain marked rows of the project.
- A session another agent opened (`ompdesk_session_open`, an Autopilot child) reads as its task until stock names it: `agentTaskTitle` (`shared/agent-control.ts`: the body's `Title:` line by `assignmentTitle`, else its first line) of the indexed first message in `sessionDisplayTitle`, and before the index lists it the tab's `task` (`IpcTabInfo.task`), never "New session". The task rides the spawn (`SpawnRequest.task` → `IpcSpawnTabPayload.task` → `SidecarPool.acquire`), so the tab's first status already carries it: set after the spawn, the first `TAB_STATUS` went out without it and a phone's row read "New session" for a frame (`e2e/mobile-autopilot-rows.e2e.ts` caught it 1 in 60). Only a tab that already exists (one a relaunch restored, named from its saved session by `#nameUntitled`) gets its task afterwards (`SidecarPool.setTabTask`). Pinned by `sidecar-pool.test.ts` ("from the first status any window hears") and `agent-control/host.test.ts`.
- Tests: `project-origin.test.ts`, `origin.test.ts` (real temp repos: main, linked, detached, bare, removed), `Sidebar.test.tsx` "worktree sessions", the run row in its project's group in `e2e/autopilot-cycles.e2e.ts` and `e2e/mobile-autopilot-rows.e2e.ts`, and the no-`gui-2026`/`gui-agent` assertions in `e2e/autopilot-cycles.e2e.ts` and `e2e/agent-review.e2e.ts`.

## Panels, sidebar and work views

### History, stats, compaction limits

- Composer history uses stock `HistoryStorage` through the guarded bridge methods `history_recent`, `history_search`, `history_record`. Local tabs share history with this machine's terminal omp; remote tabs use the paired host's history and never import client prefs. Legacy `inputHistory` prefs migrate oldest-first on the first successful local bridge load, then are deleted (`e2e/history-migration.e2e.ts`). Stock `shouldSkipHistory` keeps secrets out of SQLite. Transient recall and Up/Down draft restore stay renderer-local.
- Compaction-limit commit/prefill uses stock `config/compaction-threshold` helpers through `compaction_limits`; typing never waits for bridge validation.
- Stats: `resources/gui-bridge/stats-host.ts` runs the user's omp-stats server (stock `omp stats` always opens a browser) and adds the paginated `/api/stats/requests` route stock lacks. It also starts stock's live ingest (`omp-stats/live` `statsLive().start()`: a full sync at start and every 5 min, plus each transcript a session writes, 800 ms after it), because stock's server starts it only when a dashboard page subscribes to `/api/events`, which `omp stats` always opens and the GUI never does: without it `~/.omp/stats.db` stopped at 15 Oct 2026 for five days while sessions ran. The host attaches its stdin shutdown before any startup await, so a start that never finishes cannot orphan it (22 such hosts were seen running for days). Pinned by `stats-host-stock.test.ts` (installed omp: rows on disk and a reply written after start reach `/api/stats/requests` with no page open) and `stats-host.test.ts`.

### Side panel

- `components/layout/PanelContainer.tsx`: header = view title, the four view icons (Terminal, Changes, Preview, Files), close; a phone's tab bar holds the same four. `PANEL_TABS` is those four; a subagent's transcript is the panel's one other view (opened from its row). **The panel has no View options list** (the user's call, 21 Oct 2026: it held too much). The other session views, mapped by who opens them and what already shows the same data, with their data and scope unchanged:
  - Background tasks, the plan document (`PlanDockCard presentation="sheet"`: the latest plan with or without plan mode; the dock card shows it while plan mode is on), Logs, Session tree (`panels/SessionTreeSheet.tsx`, one view, the keyboard its own while open) and Work tree open as **sheets** (`useUiStore.openViewSheet`, `VIEW_SHEETS`; `layout/ViewSheetHost.tsx` mounts the lazy `layout/ViewSheets.tsx` inside the runtime of the tab focused when it opened, so a paired host's tab reads its host exactly as the panel did, whatever the focus does next). Entries: palette `jobs`, `logs`, `plan-document`, `tree`, `work-tree`; native menu Jobs and Session tree; the title bar's session menu (tree); the work board's job rows and the dock island (jobs); the board's Child sessions section's last row (`data-board-work-tree`); Settings › Updates & about › diagnostics' Live log (`data-diagnostics-live-log`); the sidebar's Background tasks pin.
  - Queue: the composer's queue pill and popover run the same `useQueueActions` on the same tab (edit in the field, move lanes, remove, Clear all, and per lane `data-lane-clear`).
  - Memory: Settings › Memory (the same `window.omp.aiMemory`, this computer's store; the old view never read a tab or a host), palette `memory`.
  - Retired cleanly (`src/main/retired-prefs.ts` `retirePanelViews`): a stored `defaultPanelTab` among the old views is forgotten (Changes), `keymapOverrides` for `panel.jobs|plan|logs|queue|tree|memory` dropped (they had no default chords); an agent's `ompdesk_app ui.panel` takes the four views and the five sheet ids (`AGENT_PANEL_TABS`, `AGENT_VIEW_SHEETS`). Pinned by `ViewSheetHost.test.tsx` (local and paired host tab, the tab kept when the focus moves), `PanelContainer.test.tsx`, `retired-prefs.test.ts`, `ComposerQueue.test.tsx`, `use-queue-actions.test.tsx`, `SessionTreeSheet.test.tsx`, `PlanDockCard.sheet.test.tsx`; captures `OMP_GUI_PANEL_COMPRESS_SHOTS=<dir>` with `e2e/panel-compress-captures.e2e.ts` (dev harness, before/after × light/dark × en/ko).
- There is no Autopilot view: a run lives on the work board (see Autopilot › Overview). A view's own actions sit in its toolbar row, never in the header.
- Terminal reuses stock `@oh-my-pi/pi-natives` `PtySession` inside the stock helper's terminals worker (`resources/gui-bridge/stock-helper/terminals.ts`, `src/main/terminal/`): no second native addon, one `TerminalService` per worker (overlapping first requests share its creation), stdin EOF kills every PTY. A paired host's tab gets the host's terminals (`terminal.request`/`terminal.shells` in `remote/host-services.ts`, disposed with the host tab).
- The agent reads a tab's terminals through the read-only `gui_terminal_read` host tool (stock `set_host_tools` on every ready sidecar, answered in `ipc.ts`, `src/main/terminal/agent-tool.ts`).
- Preview: a main-managed `WebContentsView` (`src/main/preview/`) on a non-persistent partition with sandbox/contextIsolation, no preload, http(s) only; permissions, downloads and new windows refused; remote-tab loopback URLs blocked.
- **Background jobs are stock's tree**: bridge `composer.jobs` reads `getAsyncJobTree()` (the session's jobs and every subagent's, stock's recent limit of 10) and lays it out with stock `pi-tui/overlays/job-tree` `buildJobTree` (registered in omp's bundle, so no `COPY_SAFE` entry; `resources/gui-bridge/jobs-core.ts` makes the rows, `jobs-contracts.ts` is the contract), each job with stock `inspectAsyncJob`'s output. A row carries `depth` and `cancellable` (stock's rule: `ownerId` absent or the tree's `rootId`; `cancelAsyncJob` refuses any other job and stock's jobs sheet offers Cancel on those alone, so a subagent's job stops with the task job that runs the subagent); an agent row heads a subagent whose task job is no longer listed. `get_jobs` returns `{ rows }`; `use-background-jobs.ts` stays the one poll and derives the running shells/evals (nested ones included) for the work board, dock island and composer cue; the Background tasks sheet (`panels/JobsPanel.tsx`, `panels/jobs-tree.ts`) indents nested rows under their subagent. Pinned by `bridge-jobs-core.test.ts`, `bridge-jobs-tree-stock.test.ts` (installed omp: nested job listed under its agent, inspected, cancel refused), `jobs-tree.test.ts`, `JobsPanel.test.tsx`. Fake omp `OMP_GUI_TEST_JOB_TREE=1`, dev harness `?jobs=tree`, opt-in captures `OMP_GUI_JOBS_TREE_SHOTS=<dir>` (`e2e/jobs-tree-captures.e2e.ts`).
- Changes polling (`panels/RepositoryChanges.tsx`, `get_git_fingerprint` in `local/git/changes.ts`): an open list asks git every 3 s only for a fingerprint (one `status --porcelain=v2 --branch -z --untracked-files=all`, whose `branch.oid` is the HEAD oid, plus an in-process `lstat` of each listed path so a second edit of a modified file moves it). The panel passes the worktree `root` its last full read reported, so `rev-parse --show-toplevel` runs only before the first read or when that root does not hold the tab's folder. 1 git process per unchanged tick vs 9 for a full `get_git_changes` (counted by trace2 in `changes.test.ts`). Only a changed fingerprint reloads the list; the open diff stays until its replacement arrives; a hidden window clears the timer; a failed reload keeps its fingerprint as baseline and waits for the checkout to change. The status runs with `--show-stash` (its `# stash <n>` header, same process; a git older than 2.35 refuses it once and is asked without), so a stash made or removed moves the fingerprint and the bar's stash count. Known gap: any other operation with no status change does not move it (the refresh button reads it).
- Every `git`/`gh` child gets `GIT_OPTIONAL_LOCKS=0` (`execBin`), so a status refresh never takes `.git/index.lock` from the agent's own git. On macOS GUI git calls run the developer dir's git directly (`gitBin` in `exec.ts`), never xcrun's `/usr/bin/git` trampoline (~3.4 ms CPU per call).
- Unsaved Files edits belong to their tab: `closeTab` on the tab named by `fileEditTabId` while `fileEditDirty` does not close it but raises the editor's discard confirm (`holdTabClose` → `pendingFileSwitch`). "Discard" sets `discardedForTabClose` and the side panel then closes the tab; Cancel keeps both.
- Subagent view (`panels/SubagentPanel.tsx`): a sidebar subagent row dragged onto a session pane (`omp:subagent-drag`, taken by `SplitWorkspace`) or its menu's "Open in side panel" sets `useUiStore.panelTab = "subagent"` + `panelSubagent {tabId, agentId}`; the view reads the transcript and steers through the owning tab's runtime. It is not in `PANEL_TABS` (default-view setting, `panel.*` shortcuts); panes stay main-owned omp tabs.
  - The transcript (`SubagentTranscript.tsx`, shared with `SubagentTranscriptSheet`) is the main transcript's own components, never a second renderer: `buildHistoryRows`/`reuseTranscriptRows` drawn by `MessageBubble`, `ProcessGroup` (compact work card, or per-message tool cards by `transcriptDetail`) and `ReadGroupCard` under `.omp-transcript-editorial.omp-subagent-conversation` (`styles/subagent-view.css`; insets follow the panel width by container query; code and tables scroll inside their block).
  - It runs in a derived `SessionRuntime` of the owning tab with private tools/messages/session stores (`hydrateMessages`, `turnIsLive` while the agent runs), so the owner's streams and goal never reach the cards. `use-subagent-transcript.ts` reads by byte offset and follows a running agent (progress frame or 4 s poll; a page with nothing new keeps the same array); `use-stick-to-bottom.ts` is ChatStream's gesture-vetoed follow plus "Jump to latest". Header is one line (provider mark + agent, status, tokens, clock, open full / stop / copy); the steer box reuses `.omp-composer-box` and `.omp-composer-action`.
  - Tests: `SubagentPanel.test.tsx`, `SidebarSubagents.test.tsx`; `e2e/subagent-view.e2e.ts` (`fixture:subagents:transcript`/`:append`, light/dark × en/ko × 380/520 px, 320 px overflow; `OMP_GUI_SUBAGENT_SHOTS=<dir>` writes captures).

### Memory (ai-memory)

- Memory is ai-memory: `src/main/ai-memory/`, contract `src/shared/ai-memory.ts`, pref `aiMemory` default **on** (only a stored `false` turns it off, `aiMemoryEnabled`). Settings › Memory (`settings/memory/MemorySection.tsx`) is the ONLY memory setting. The name in every UI string is just "Memory"/"메모리", never "ai-memory"; no link to the project's repo or docs, no credits in the UI. The licence notice lives in `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt` and `LICENSES/` only; Settings › Updates & about › This computer lists it as one of OMPdesk's own components (`settings/memory/MemoryComponentRow.tsx`: version, status, "updated with OMPdesk", no update control).
- Stock's `MemoryBackend` (`memory.backend` rows, the `memory` model role row via `HIDDEN_ROLE_IDS`, the bridge's `get_memory_report`/`memory_*` methods) is gone from OMPdesk. Stock keeps `memory.backend` `off` for RPC hosts itself (`protocolDefault: ["rpc", "acp"]`), so nothing is held as a runtime default.
- **No side panel view**: Memory lives on Settings › Memory alone (status, counts, pages by kind, handoffs, the web console); palette command `cmd.memory` ("Memory") opens it. The side panel's glance (status, the newest pages, open handoffs) was removed on 21 Oct 2026 (see Side panel).
- **OMPdesk's own source, built by OMPdesk** (ai-memory, MIT, [akitaonrails/ai-memory](https://github.com/akitaonrails/ai-memory)): it lives in `native/memory/` (a `git subtree add --squash` of upstream tag v3.1.1; tag and commit in `native/memory/UPSTREAM.md` and `native/memory/ompdesk.json`; the upstream `LICENSE` is kept; `.github/`, `companions/`, `docker/`, `nix/`, `flake.*` and its AGENTS/CLAUDE files were removed on import). Edit it like our code. No release asset of the upstream is downloaded or pinned anywhere (`sync:ai-memory`, `bump:ai-memory` and the pin file are gone).
  - `bun run build:memory` (`scripts/build-memory.ts`) runs the upstream's own release build (`cargo build --locked --release -p ai-memory-cli`, default features, Cargo.lock as committed, `native/memory/rust-toolchain.toml` = 1.95) for the host (macOS arm64 or Windows x64) and writes the gitignored `resources/ai-memory-bin/<platform>-<arch>/ai-memory[.exe]` plus `ai-memory.build.json` (binary name, **SHA-256 recorded at build time**, version, upstream tag/commit, rustc, build time and duration). Each builder config ships that directory as `<resources>/ai-memory-bin/` outside the asar. `scripts/after-pack.cjs` `checkAiMemory` (and `packaging-config.test.ts`) fail a package without the binary and its record, with another version than `native/memory/ompdesk.json`, or whose bytes differ from the record; `scripts/mac-sign.cjs` signs the nested binary with the app's certificate; the release's `Verify bundle` verifies and runs it. Without Rust, `build:memory` prints how to install it and exits 1; the app then shows the failed phase "Memory is not built", never a crash (a dev run only reports).
  - CI: release.yml's `mac` and `win` jobs first restore the built binary and its record (`actions/cache/restore`, path `resources/ai-memory-bin`, key `ai-memory-bin-<os>-<arch>-<hashFiles native/memory/** + scripts/build-memory.ts + resources/gui-bridge/memory-prompt.ts + resources/gui-bridge/redactor.ts>`; the toolchain file and Cargo.lock sit inside native/memory, and `memory-prompt.ts` and `redactor.ts` (`EMBEDDED_SOURCES` in build-memory.ts) are the files outside it the CLI compiles in: `install_hooks.rs` embeds them with `include_str!` into the omp extension it generates, so a new input outside native/memory joins every key, `EMBEDDED_SOURCES` and ci.yml's change test). A hit runs only `bun run build:memory -- --check` (the record matches native/memory/ompdesk.json, this OS/arch and the SHA-256 over the current `EMBEDDED_SOURCES` it records as `embeddedSha256`, the binary's SHA-256 is recomputed against it, its `--version` runs; after-pack checks the packaged bytes again and mac-sign.cjs signs them at packaging as on a build). A miss installs Rust 1.95 (`dtolnay/rust-toolchain`), restores `Swatinem/rust-cache` (workspace `native/memory`; dependencies only, so the 11 workspace crates compile), runs `bun run build:memory` and saves the cache right after the build, so a later failing step keeps it. A miss costs 9m23s on mac and 7m13s on win (dry run 39229484141; almost all of it the `ai-memory-cli` crate's thin-LTO link with `codegen-units = 1`; 3m41s on an M-series Mac). ci.yml's manual Windows jobs do the same, and its `memory` job (macOS) builds only when `native/memory/**`, `scripts/build-memory.ts`, `resources/gui-bridge/memory-prompt.ts` or `resources/gui-bridge/redactor.ts` changed (then also `licenses:check`) and saves under the same key, so a release after that push hits the cache.
  - **Merging upstream**: `git subtree pull --prefix native/memory https://github.com/akitaonrails/ai-memory <tag> --squash`; delete again what we removed when it comes back (`.github/`, `companions/`, `docker/`, `nix/`, `flake.*`, `AGENTS.md`, `CLAUDE.md`), keep our own edits, update `UPSTREAM.md`, `ompdesk.json` and (if it moved) the version in `native/memory/Cargo.toml`.
